Evidence

angular-mass-remainder-20260915T053108_249579Z.json

IDWT's physical premise is one infinite-dimensional manifold with all sectors nested as submanifolds. Particle depth counts macroscopic spatial dimensions; matrix and field-component counts in a calculation add no physical dimensions.

Recorded data and provenance for the associated calculation.

Download recorded JSON

experimentangular-mass-remainder-bound
created_utc20260915T053108_249579Z
statuspass
scopeAn analytic infinite-complement bound evaluated with refined radial coefficient estimates; these estimates are not interval-arithmetic enclosures. Large hopping values demonstrate a sufficient regime, not selected physical lengths.
checks_total588
elapsed_seconds3.083734195912257
